 266: 
 267:     def consume_from_stockpile(self, resource_type: str, amount: float) -> bool:
 268:         """All-or-nothing consume. Return True iff sufficient stockpile."""
 269:         current = self._stockpile.get(resource_type, 0.0)
 270:         if current >= amount:
 271:             self._stockpile[resource_type] = current - amount
 272:             return True
 273:         return False
 274: 
 275:     def has_stockpile(self, costs: dict) -> bool:
 276:         """True iff every resource->amount in `costs` is satisfied."""
 277:         return all(
 278:             self._stockpile.get(rt, 0.0) >= amt for rt, amt in costs.items()
 279:         )
 280: 
 281:     def get_stockpile(self, resource_type: str) -> float:
 282:         """Current stockpiled amount, 0.0 if absent."""
 283:         return self._stockpile.get(resource_type, 0.0)
 284: 
 285:     # --- IProductionResourceSource (PROJ-436 Phase 8) ---
 286:     # Delegators over the stockpile API above so ProductionEngine reads
 287:     # through one uniform protocol satisfied by both Planet and Fleet
 288:     # â€” no context_type dispatch. Stockpile API above stays as the
 289:     # public surface for transfer_branches.py / IStockpileHolder.
 290: 
 291:     def production_has_resources(self, costs: Dict[str, float]) -> bool:
 292:         return self.has_stockpile(costs)
 293: 
 294:     def production_get_resource(self, resource_type: str) -> float:
 295:         return self.get_stockpile(resource_type)
 296: 
 297:     def production_consume_resource(self, resource_type: str, amount: float) -> bool:
 298:         return self.consume_from_stockpile(resource_type, amount)
 299: 
 300:     # --- IStagingYardHolder protocol (PROJ-372) ---
 301: 
 302:     def get_staging_mass(self) -> float:
 303:         """Total mass of items currently in the staging yard."""
 304:         return sum(item.get('mass', 0.0) for item in self._staging_yard)
 305: 
 306:     def add_to_staging_yard(self, item: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
 307:         """Add an item; return False on insufficient capacity."""
 308:         item_mass = item.get('mass', 0.0)
 309:         if self.max_staging_mass > 0 and self.get_staging_mass() + item_mass > self.max_staging_mass:
 310:             return False
 311:         self._staging_yard.append(item)
 312:         return True
 313: 
 314:     def remove_from_staging_yard(self, index: int) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
 315:         """Remove and return the item at `index`, or None if out of range."""
 316:         if 0 <= index < len(self._staging_yard):
 317:             return self._staging_yard.pop(index)
 318:         return None
 319: 
 320:     def can_build_type(self, vehicle_type: str) -> bool:
 321:         """Check if this planet can build the given vehicle type.
 322: 
 323:         PROJ-372 Phase 2: 1-line facade delegating to PlanetQueryService.
 324:         """
 325:         from game.strategy.services.planet_query_service import PlanetQueryService
 326:         return PlanetQueryService.can_build_type(self, vehicle_type)
 327: 
 328:     # --- IOrderable (PROJ-238 â€” unified with Fleet) ---
 329: 
 330:     def get_current_order(self) -> Optional['Order']:
 331:         """First order in the queue, or None."""
 332:         return self.orders[0] if self.orders else None
 333: 
 334:     def pop_order(self) -> Optional['Order']:
 335:         """Pop and return the first order, or None."""
 336:         return self.orders.pop(0) if self.orders else None
 337: 
 338:     def add_order(self, order: 'Order', index: Optional[int] = None) -> None:
 339:         """Add `order` at `index` (or append if None)."""
 340:         if index is not None:
 341:             self.orders.insert(index, order)
 342:         else:
 343:             self.orders.append(order)
 344: 
 345:     def clear_orders(self) -> None:
 346:         """Remove all orders from the planet's queue."""
 347:         self.orders.clear()
 348: 
 349:     def add_production(self, design_id: str, turns: int, vehicle_type: str = "ship") -> None:
 350:         """Add a build item; vehicle_type in {ship, fighter, satellite, complex}."""
 351:         self.construction_queue.append({
 352:             "design_id": design_id,
 353:             "type": vehicle_type,
 354:             "turns_remaining": turns,
 355:         })
 356: 
 357:     def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 358:         """Serialize planet to dict for save system. PROJ-372 Phase 2: facade
 359:         delegating to ``planet_serde.planet_to_dict``."""
 360:         from game.strategy.data.planet_serde import planet_to_dict
 361:         return planet_to_dict(self)
 362: 
 363:     @classmethod
 364:     def from_dict(cls, data: dict) -> 'Planet':
 365:         """Deserialize planet from save data. PROJ-372 Phase 2: facade
 366:         delegating to ``planet_serde.planet_from_dict_kwargs``."""
 367:         from game.strategy.data.planet_serde import planet_from_dict_kwargs
 368:         return cls(**planet_from_dict_kwargs(data))

 392:     def get_hp_percentage(self) -> float:
 393:         """Get current HP as percentage of max."""
 394:         if self.current_hp is None:
 395:             return 1.0

 397:         if max_hp <= 0:
 398:             return 0.0
 399:         return self.current_hp / max_hp

 423: 
 424:     def get_all_resource_costs_per_hex(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
 425:         """Per-hex consumption costs (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
 426:         return self._resource_mgr.get_all_resource_costs_per_hex()
 427: 
 428:     def get_all_resource_costs_per_turn(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
 429:         """Per-turn consumption costs (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
 430:         return self._resource_mgr.get_all_resource_costs_per_turn()
 431: 
 432:     # --- Cargo / carried-vehicle helpers ---
 433:     #
 434:     # PROJ-425 Phase 6 (TD-06 batch 5c): the method forwarders for
 435:     # cargo queries / mutators, carried-vehicle queries, and pod-storage
 436:     # helpers were demolished. Callers go through ``ship._cargo_mgr``
 437:     # (see :class:`ShipCargoManager`). The ``bay_current_mass`` and
 438:     # ``bay_capacity_mass`` *properties* below are kept as small
 439:     # read-only entity attributes â€” they read naturally as
 440:     # ship-instance state (parallel to ``design_name`` /
 441:     # ``hull_class``) and are referenced by tests as such.
 442: 
 443:     @property
 444:     def bay_current_mass(self) -> float:
 445:         """PROJ-FMS-A: runtime vehicle-bay usage on this ship.
 446: 
 447:         Mirrors the ``bay_capacity_mass`` design stat. Computed at the
 448:         strategy layer because it depends on the actual contents of
 449:         ``bay_inventory.bay`` (simulation ``Ship`` cannot see those).
 450:         """
 451:         return self._cargo_mgr.get_carried_vehicle_mass()
 452: 
 453:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 454:     # PROJ-436 Phase 3a â€” unified Container projections.
 455:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 456:     #
 457:     # These are read-only snapshot views over the legacy
 458:     # `consumable_levels` and `cargo_contents` dataclass fields. Later
 459:     # sub-phases of Phase 3 migrate callers off the legacy fields; the
 460:     # final cutover deletes both fields and these views become the
 461:     # canonical read API. Until then, the views serve callers that
 462:     # want to work in the unified Container abstraction without
 463:     # forcing a wholesale migration.
 464:     #
 465:     # Snapshot semantics: mutations on the returned Container are NOT
 466:     # propagated back to the ship. Treat as a read view.
 467: 
 468:     def consumable_container(self) -> 'Container':
 469:         """Container snapshot of this ship's `consumable_levels`.
 470: 
 471:         Every entry in `consumable_levels` (resource_name -> amount)
 472:         appears in the resource slice. Unknown resource ids (not in
 473:         `ResourceCatalog`) raise via `Container.add_resource` â€”
 474:         consumable_levels keys are assumed to be canonical resource ids
 475:         per the existing component-driven loading code.
 476:         """
 477:         from game.strategy.data.container import (
 478:             Container,
 479:             ContainerPolicy,
 480:         )
 481:         from game.strategy.data.containable import (
 482:             ContainableKind,
 483:             ResourceContainable,
 484:         )
 485: 
 486:         policy = ContainerPolicy(
 487:             allowed_kinds=frozenset({ContainableKind.RESOURCE}),
 488:             allowed_type_ids=None,
 489:         )
 490:         c = Container(capacity_mass=float("inf"), policy=policy)
 491:         for resource_id, amount in self.consumable_levels.items():
 492:             c.add(ResourceContainable(resource_id), amount)
 493:         return c
 494: 
 495:     def cargo_container(self) -> 'Container':
 496:         """Container snapshot of this ship's `cargo_contents`.
 497: 
 498:         Translation rules for cargo_contents keys:
 499:         - "passengers" -> population slice (default species id).
 500:         - Known resource ids (in `ResourceCatalog`) -> resource slice.
 501:         - Other keys -> logged and skipped (no representation in the
 502:           snapshot). The most common skipped keys are "drop_pod" and
 503:           "vehicle" which live in `bay_inventory` instead.
 504:         """
 505:         from game.core.resources import ResourceCatalog
 506:         from game.strategy.data.container import (
 507:             Container,
 508:             ContainerPolicy,
 509:             _get_resource_catalog,
 510:         )
 511:         from game.strategy.data.containable import (
 512:             ContainableKind,
 513:             PopulationContainable,
 514:             ResourceContainable,
 515:         )
 516: 
 517:         catalog: ResourceCatalog = _get_resource_catalog()
 518:         policy = ContainerPolicy(
 519:             allowed_kinds=frozenset({
 520:                 ContainableKind.RESOURCE,
 521:                 ContainableKind.POPULATION,
 522:             }),
 523:             allowed_type_ids=None,
 524:         )
 525:         c = Container(capacity_mass=float("inf"), policy=policy)
 526:         for key, amount in self.cargo_contents.items():
 527:             if key == "passengers":
 528:                 c.add(PopulationContainable("default"), int(amount))
 529:             elif catalog.has(key):
 530:                 c.add(ResourceContainable(key), float(amount))
 531:             else:
 532:                 logger.debug(
 533:                     "cargo_container: skipping cargo_contents key %r "
 534:                     "(not a known resource id or 'passengers')",
 535:                     key,
 536:                 )
 537:         return c
 538: 
 539:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 540:     # PROJ-431 Phase 1f: bay_inventory IS the canonical storage.
 541:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 542:     #
 543:     # The typed :class:`BayInventory` (``bay: list[CarriedVehicle]`` +
 544:     # ``pods: list[DropPod]``) is now a real dataclass field on this
 545:     # entity, not a projection over the legacy mixed-shape list. The
 546:     # earlier ``carried_items`` field was removed; what remains here is
 547:     # a backward-compatible property/setter exposing the legacy
 548:     # dict-list shape exclusively for **test infrastructure** that still
 549:     # pokes ``ship.carried_items.append({...})`` directly. Production
 550:     # code uses :attr:`bay_inventory` and :meth:`set_bay_inventory`.
 551: 
 552:     def set_bay_inventory(self, bay_inventory: 'BayInventory') -> None:
 553:         """Replace this ship's typed bay inventory wholesale.
 554: 
 555:         Phase 1f: this is a thin attribute setter retained as a stable
 556:         write surface for callers that produced a new
 557:         :class:`BayInventory` value (e.g. ``ShipCargoManager`` loaders /
 558:         unloaders, FMS handlers). Callers that just mutate the lists in
 559:         place do not need to call this â€” the underlying field is now
 560:         owned by this entity directly.
 561:         """
 562:         if not isinstance(bay_inventory, BayInventory):
 563:             raise TypeError(
 564:                 f"set_bay_inventory expects BayInventory, got "
 565:                 f"{type(bay_inventory).__name__}"
 566:             )
 567:         self.bay_inventory = bay_inventory
 568: 
 569:     # PROJ-436 Phase 9: ``carried_items`` property + ``_CarriedItemsProxy``
 570:     # test shim deleted. Callers route through ``bay_inventory.bay`` /
 571:     # ``bay_inventory.pods`` / ``set_bay_inventory(...)`` directly.
 572:

 667:         self,

 706:     # --- Serializer Methods (PROJ-425 Phase 5d: thin shims) ---

 717:     # `.to_json(ship)` / `.from_json(s)` / `.clone(ship)` access. Tests

 722:         """Serialize for save game."""

 762:         """Create a deep copy of this instance (for hypothetical battles)."""

 766:     def __repr__(self) -> str:
 767:         hp_status = f"{self.current_hp}HP" if self.current_hp is not None else "Full"
 768:         status = "DESTROYED" if not self.is_alive else ("DERELICT" if self.is_derelict else "OK")
 769:         return f"ShipInstance({self.name}, {hp_status}, {status})"

  73:     def is_eliminated(self) -> bool:
  74:         """Return True when this empire owns no fleets, no colonies, and
  75:         no deployed groups.
  76: 
  77:         Pure read; no side effects. Used by the hot-seat turn-start hook
  78:         (issue #25) to detect defeat, remove the empire from the rotation,
  79:         and fire a one-shot defeat modal. AI/non-human empires use the
  80:         same predicate.
  81: 
  82:         PROJ-431 Phase 2: deployed groups (e.g. orphan ``MineGroup``s)
  83:         keep an empire alive â€” a minefield on the map is still an asset
  84:         on the strategic surface, even after every fleet/colony is lost.
  85:         """
  86:         return (
  87:             len(self.fleets) == 0
  88:             and len(self.colonies) == 0
  89:             and len(self.deployed_groups) == 0
  90:         )

  92:     # --- Deployed groups (PROJ-431 Phase 2) ---

  94:     def add_deployed_group(self, group: DeployedGroup) -> None:
  95:         """Attach a deployed group to this empire.

  97:         Takes ownership: ``group.owner_id`` is rewritten to this empire's
  98:         id, matching ``add_fleet``'s contract. Dedupes â€” adding the same
  99:         group twice is a no-op.
 100:         """
 101:         if group in self.deployed_groups:
 102:             return
 103:         self.deployed_groups.append(group)
 104:         group.owner_id = self.id

 107:         """Detach a deployed group. Returns True if it was present."""
 108:         if group in self.deployed_groups:

 110:             return True
 111:         return False

 142:     def add_fleet(self, fleet) -> None:

 155:         """Remove fleet from empire and auto-unregister from galaxy.
 156:

 165:             fleet: Fleet to remove.
 166:             event_bus: Optional EventBus for structured event logging.
 167:         """

 177:                             EventType.FLEET_JOIN_CANCELLED,
 178:                             category=EventCategory.FLEET_OPERATIONS,
 179:                             empire_id=pursuer.owner_id,
 180:                             message=f"Fleet {pursuer.id} join order cancelled: target Fleet {fleet.id} destroyed",
 181:                             fleet_id=pursuer.id,
 182:                             target_fleet_id=fleet.id,

 214:         next_serial = current + 1

 226:     # --- Resource Economy Methods ---
 227: 
 228:     @property
 229:     def resource_pool(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
 230:         """Read-only aggregate of all colony stockpiles.
 231: 
 232:         PROJ-436 Phase 5: pure aggregation query. Walks every colony's
 233:         ``stockpile`` and sums by ``resource_id``. The legacy
 234:         ``_fleet_resource_pool`` summand has been deleted along with
 235:         the durable field â€” fleet construction draws from the build-
 236:         location's container, not an empire-level pool.
 237: 
 238:         Per Phase 0 D2 default this stays an uncached pure query; if
 239:         post-Phase-5 profiling shows the aggregation is hot at large-
 240:         empire scale, caching with explicit invalidation (PROJ-293
 241:         pattern) can land as a sibling sub-phase.
 242: 
 243:         Used for UI display and economy reporting.
 244:         """
 245:         totals: Dict[str, float] = {}
 246:         for colony in self.colonies:
 247:             for res, amount in colony.stockpile.items():
 248:                 totals[res] = totals.get(res, 0.0) + amount
 249:         return totals
 250: 
 251:     def has_resources(self, costs: dict) -> bool:
 252:         """Check if the empire has all required resources (aggregate).
 253: 
 254:         Reads ``self.resource_pool`` â€” i.e. the sum across colony
 255:         stockpiles. Used by UI affordability checks; production code
 256:         consults the build-location's container directly.
 257: 
 258:         Args:
 259:             costs: Dict mapping resource_type -> required amount.
 260: 
 261:         Returns:
 262:             True if all resources are available.
 263:         """
 264:         pool = self.resource_pool  # computed aggregate
 265:         for resource_type, amount in costs.items():
 266:             if pool.get(resource_type, 0.0) < amount:
 267:                 return False
 268:         return True
 269: 
 270:     def get_resource(self, resource_type: str) -> float:
 271:         """Get current amount of a resource type (aggregate).
 272: 
 273:         Returns 0.0 if the resource type is not in the pool.
 274:         """
 275:         return self.resource_pool.get(resource_type, 0.0)
 276: 
 277:     # --- Population Queries (PROJ-287) ---
 278: 
 279:     def resident_species(self) -> Set[str]:
 280:         """Return the set of race_ids with count >= 1 anywhere in this
 281:         empire's colonies (PROJ-287).
 282: 
 283:         Canonical "species living in this empire" set â€” consumed by UI
 284:         that iterates per-species (e.g. PROJ-290's uncolonized-habitability
 285:         display). A species is included if ANY colony has at least one
 286:         population unit; species with count=0 on every colony are excluded
 287:         as extinct.
 288: 
 289:         Not cached â€” empires have O(10-100) colonies Ã— O(1-5) species, so
 290:         the iteration is cheap compared to the invalidation complexity a
 291:         cache would require (population growth, extinction, colonization).
 292:         """
 293:         species: Set[str] = set()
 294:         for colony in self.colonies:
 295:             for pop in colony.populations:
 296:                 if pop.count >= 1:
 297:                     species.add(pop.race_id)
 298:         return species

 300:     def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 301:         """
 302:         Serialize Empire to dict.
 303: 
 304:         Stores colony IDs only (not full Planet objects) to avoid circular references.
 305:         Planets will be resolved during load via galaxy.get_planet_by_id().
 306:         """
 307:         data = {
 308:             'id': self.id,
 309:             'name': self.name,
 310:             'color': self.color,
 311:             'theme_path': self.theme_path,
 312:             'empire_theme_id': self.empire_theme_id,
 313:             'colony_ids': [p.id for p in self.colonies],  # Store IDs only
 314:             'fleets': [f.to_dict() for f in self.fleets],
 315:             # Deployed groups serialise as a sibling collection.
 316:             # Polymorphic dispatch on the ``type`` field at load time
 317:             # picks the correct DeployedGroup subclass.
 318:             'deployed_groups': [g.to_dict() for g in self.deployed_groups],
 319:             'built_ship_designs': sorted(self.built_ship_designs),
 320:             '_next_fleet_display_number': self._next_fleet_display_number,
 321:             '_design_serial_counters': self._design_serial_counters,
 322:             'max_storage': dict(self.max_storage),
 323:         }
 324:         # Include race visual identity if set (optional fields)
 325:         if self.flag_id:
 326:             data['flag_id'] = self.flag_id
 327:         if self.portrait_id:
 328:             data['portrait_id'] = self.portrait_id
 329:         # Include full race config if set
 330:         if self.race_config is not None:
 331:             data['race_config'] = self.race_config.to_dict()
 332:         return data

 334:     @classmethod
 335:     def from_dict(
 336:         cls,
 337:         data: dict,
 338:         galaxy=None,
 339:         registries: Optional['GameRegistries'] = None
 340:     ) -> 'Empire':
 341:         """
 342:         Deserialize Empire from dict.
 343: 
 344:         Args:
 345:             data: Saved empire data
 346:             galaxy: Galaxy instance for resolving planet references (required)
 347:             registries: Optional GameRegistries for DI. If provided, fleets
 348:                 and ships will use these registries.
 349: 
 350:         Returns:
 351:             Reconstructed Empire with colonies resolved
 352: 
 353:         Raises:
 354:             PersistenceException: If required keys missing
 355:         """
 356:         require_keys(data, ['id', 'name', 'color'], 'Empire')
 357: 
 358:         from game.strategy.data.fleet import Fleet
 359:         from game.strategy.data.race_config import RaceConfig
 360: 
 361:         # Deserialize race_config if present (with error handling)
 362:         race_config = None
 363:         if 'race_config' in data:
 364:             race_config = safe_from_dict(RaceConfig.from_dict, data['race_config'], 'Empire.race_config')
 365: 
 366:         empire = cls(
 367:             empire_id=data['id'],
 368:             name=data['name'],
 369:             color=data['color'],
 370:             theme_path=data.get('theme_path'),
 371:             empire_theme_id=data.get('empire_theme_id', 'Federation'),
 372:             flag_id=data.get('flag_id', ''),
 373:             portrait_id=data.get('portrait_id', ''),
 374:             race_config=race_config
 375:         )
 376: 
 377:         # Restore built_ship_designs set
 378:         empire.built_ship_designs = set(data.get('built_ship_designs', []))
 379: 
 380:         # Restore fleet display number counter
 381:         empire._next_fleet_display_number = data.get('_next_fleet_display_number', 1)
 382: 
 383:         # Restore ship serial counters
 384:         empire._design_serial_counters = data.get('_design_serial_counters', {})
 385: 
 386:         empire.max_storage = data.get('max_storage', {})
 387: 
 388:         # PROJ-251: Strict deserialization â€” corrupt fleets fail the load
 389:         # PROJ-211: Pass registries for DI
 390:         empire.fleets = []
 391:         for i, fleet_data in enumerate(data.get('fleets', [])):
 392:             try:
 393:                 fleet = Fleet.from_dict(fleet_data, registries=registries)
 394:                 fleet.owner_id = empire.id
 395:                 empire.fleets.append(fleet)
 396:             except (PersistenceException, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as e:
 397:                 raise PersistenceException(
 398:                     f"Corrupt fleet data at index {i} in empire '{data.get('id', '?')}'",
 399:                     code=ErrorCode.CORRUPT_DATA.value,
 400:                     context={"empire_id": data.get('id'), "fleet_index": i, "original_error": str(e)}
 401:                 ) from e
 402: 
 403:         # PROJ-431 Phase 2: restore deployed groups.
 404:         empire.deployed_groups = []
 405:         for i, dg_data in enumerate(data.get('deployed_groups', []) or []):
 406:             try:
 407:                 group = DeployedGroup.from_dict(dg_data)
 408:                 group.owner_id = empire.id
 409:                 empire.deployed_groups.append(group)
 410:             except (PersistenceException, KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as e:
 411:                 raise PersistenceException(
 412:                     f"Corrupt deployed_group data at index {i} in empire "
 413:                     f"'{data.get('id', '?')}'",
 414:                     code=ErrorCode.CORRUPT_DATA.value,
 415:                     context={
 416:                         "empire_id": data.get('id'),
 417:                         "deployed_group_index": i,
 418:                         "original_error": str(e),
 419:                     },
 420:                 ) from e
 421: 
 422:         # Resolve colony references via galaxy
 423:         if galaxy is not None:
 424:             for planet_id in data.get('colony_ids', []):
 425:                 planet = galaxy.get_planet_by_id(planet_id)
 426:                 if planet:
 427:                     empire.colonies.append(planet)
 428:                     planet.owner_id = empire.id
 429: 
 430:         return empire

  65:         invalidate ``HarvestingEngine``'s per-turn caches. Callers that

  81:         """

  83:             planet.facilities.remove(facility)
  84:             if empire is not None:
  85:                 empire._storage_dirty = True
  86:                 empire._booster_dirty = True
  87:             return True
  88:         return False

  99:     # --- Staging yard ---
 100:     def add_staging_item(self, planet: "Planet", item: Any) -> None:
 101:         # Delegate to existing public API (enforces capacity).
 102:         planet.add_to_staging_yard(item)

   1: """Polymorphic issuer adapter for FMS order handlers (QA Observation B).
   2: 
   3: Background â€” both fleets AND planets can now issue strategic FMS orders
   4: (``LAY_MINES``, ``LAUNCH_FIGHTERS``, ``LAUNCH_SATELLITES``,
   5: ``RECOVER_FIGHTERS``, ``RECOVER_SATELLITES``). Previously every order
   6: handler reached directly into ``fleet.ships[i].carried_items``; widening
   7: that contract one handler at a time would duplicate the new
   8: planet-staging-yard branch in five places.
   9:

  11: implementations:
  12: 
  13: - :class:`FleetShipIssuerAdapter` wraps the existing
  14:   ``(Fleet, ShipInstance)`` shape so order handlers can keep operating

 114: 
 115: def _matches(item: Any, vehicle_type: str, design_id: Optional[str]) -> bool:
 116:     """Shared filter predicate used by both adapters.
 117: 
 118:     Accepts both raw dict items (planet staging yard â€” pre-Phase-1b
 119:     substrate, still on the legacy mixed-shape list) and typed
 120:     :class:`CarriedVehicle` instances (fleet-ship bay â€” Phase-1b
 121:     substrate).
 122: 
 123:     PROJ-431 Phase 1f: the legacy ``CarriedVehicle.from_any(...)``
 124:     discriminator is gone. Replaced with an explicit isinstance check
 125:     plus a dict-shape probe against
 126:     :data:`~game.strategy.data.carried_vehicle.VALID_VEHICLE_TYPES`,
 127:     routing through ``CarriedVehicle.from_dict`` only when the shape
 128:     matches.
 129:     """
 130:     if isinstance(item, CarriedVehicle):
 131:         cv: Optional[CarriedVehicle] = item
 132:     elif isinstance(item, dict) and str(
 133:         item.get("vehicle_type", "")
 134:     ).lower() in VALID_VEHICLE_TYPES:
 135:         cv = CarriedVehicle.from_dict(item)
 136:     else:
 137:         cv = None
 138:     if cv is None or cv.vehicle_type != vehicle_type:
 139:         return False
 140:     if design_id and design_id != "auto":
 141:         if cv.design_id != design_id:
 142:             return False
 143:     return True
 144: 
 145: 
 146: def _cv_matches(cv: CarriedVehicle, vehicle_type: str, design_id: Optional[str]) -> bool:
 147:     """Typed predicate for the fleet-ship adapter's bay reads."""
 148:     if cv.vehicle_type != vehicle_type:
 149:         return False
 150:     if design_id and design_id != "auto":
 151:         if cv.design_id != design_id:
 152:             return False
 153:     return True

 156: class FleetShipIssuerAdapter:
 157:     """IIssuerAdapter wrapping a fleet + carrier ship.
 158: 
 159:     PROJ-431 Phase 1b: reads and writes route through the typed
 160:     :class:`BayInventory` substrate
 161:     (``ship.bay_inventory`` / ``ship.set_bay_inventory(...)``) instead
 162:     of the legacy ``carried_items`` mixed list. ``pop_carried`` still
 163:     returns dict items so the FMS order handlers (out of scope for 1b)
 164:     continue to operate on their existing dict contract; ``append_carried``
 165:     accepts both dict and :class:`CarriedVehicle` inputs so callers can
 166:     migrate independently.
 167:     """
 168: 
 169:     def __init__(self, fleet: "Fleet", ship: "ShipInstance") -> None:
 170:         self._fleet = fleet
 171:         self._ship = ship
 172: 
 173:     @property
 174:     def fleet(self) -> "Fleet":
 175:         return self._fleet
 176: 
 177:     @property
 178:     def ship(self) -> "ShipInstance":
 179:         return self._ship
 180: 
 181:     @property
 182:     def location(self) -> HexCoord:
 183:         return self._fleet.location
 184: 
 185:     @property
 186:     def owner_id(self) -> int:
 187:         return int(self._fleet.owner_id)
 188: 
 189:     @property
 190:     def display_label(self) -> str:
 191:         name = getattr(self._fleet, "display_name", None)
 192:         if name:
 193:             return str(name)
 194:         return f"Fleet {self._fleet.id}"
 195: 
 196:     def pop_carried(
 197:         self,
 198:         vehicle_type: str,
 199:         design_id: Optional[str],
 200:         count: Optional[int],
 201:     ) -> List[Any]:
 202:         # PROJ-431 Phase 1c: return typed ``CarriedVehicle`` instances
 203:         # straight from the typed BayInventory.bay slot. The dict-round-
 204:         # trip from Phase 1b is gone â€” FMS order handlers now consume
 205:         # the typed payload directly. Planet adapter's matching method
 206:         # still yields dicts for the staging-yard substrate (out of
 207:         # scope until 1d).
 208:         current = self._ship.bay_inventory
 209:         popped: List[CarriedVehicle] = []
 210:         remaining_bay: List[CarriedVehicle] = []
 211:         limit = count if (count is not None and count >= 0) else None
 212:         for cv in current.bay:
 213:             if limit is not None and len(popped) >= limit:
 214:                 remaining_bay.append(cv)
 215:                 continue
 216:             if _cv_matches(cv, vehicle_type, design_id):
 217:                 popped.append(cv)
 218:             else:
 219:                 remaining_bay.append(cv)
 220:         self._ship.set_bay_inventory(
 221:             BayInventory(bay=remaining_bay, pods=list(current.pods))
 222:         )
 223:         return popped
 224: 
 225:     def count_carried(
 226:         self,
 227:         vehicle_type: str,
 228:         design_id: Optional[str],
 229:     ) -> int:
 230:         return sum(
 231:             1 for cv in self._ship.bay_inventory.bay
 232:             if _cv_matches(cv, vehicle_type, design_id)
 233:         )
 234: 
 235:     def append_carried(self, items: List[Any]) -> int:
 236:         # PROJ-431 Phase 1b: route writes through the typed BayInventory.
 237:         # Items arrive from out-of-scope handlers as dicts (vehicle dicts
 238:         # or pod dicts) or as already-typed CarriedVehicle instances;
 239:         # accept both and route each into the correct typed slot.
 240:         current = self._ship.bay_inventory
 241:         new_bay = list(current.bay)
 242:         new_pods = list(current.pods)
 243:         appended = 0
 244:         for item in items:
 245:             if isinstance(item, CarriedVehicle):
 246:                 new_bay.append(item)
 247:                 appended += 1
 248:                 continue
 249:             if isinstance(item, DropPod):
 250:                 new_pods.append(item)
 251:                 appended += 1
 252:                 continue
 253:             if isinstance(item, dict):
 254:                 # PROJ-431 Phase 1f: explicit dict-shape probe replaces
 255:                 # the legacy ``CarriedVehicle.from_any`` discriminator.
 256:                 vt = str(item.get("vehicle_type", "")).lower()
 257:                 if vt in VALID_VEHICLE_TYPES:
 258:                     new_bay.append(CarriedVehicle.from_dict(item))
 259:                     appended += 1
 260:                     continue
 261:                 # Drop-pod-shaped dict (or other non-vehicle payload) â€”
 262:                 # preserve in the pods slot.
 263:                 new_pods.append(DropPod.from_dict(item))
 264:                 appended += 1
 265:         self._ship.set_bay_inventory(BayInventory(bay=new_bay, pods=new_pods))
 266:         return appended
 267: 
 268:     def append_recovered(self, vehicle: CarriedVehicle) -> bool:
 269:         cargo_mgr = getattr(self._ship, "_cargo_mgr", None)
 270:         if cargo_mgr is None:
 271:             return False
 272:         return bool(cargo_mgr.load_vehicle(vehicle))

 275: class PlanetStagingYardIssuerAdapter:
 276:     """IIssuerAdapter wrapping a planet's staging yard.
 277: 
 278:     Used when a planet's facility (with ``StrategicMineLayer`` /
 279:     ``StrategicFighterLaunch`` / ``StrategicSatelliteLaunch`` /
 280:     ``RecoverFighters`` / ``RecoverSatellites``) issues an FMS order.
 281: 
 282:     Pop/append act on :attr:`Planet.staging_yard`. ``append_recovered``
 283:     routes through :meth:`Planet.add_to_staging_yard` so the
 284:     ``max_staging_mass`` cap is honoured.
 285:     """
 286: 
 287:     def __init__(self, planet: "Planet") -> None:
 288:         self._planet = planet
 289: 
 290:     @property
 291:     def planet(self) -> "Planet":
 292:         return self._planet
 293: 
 294:     @property
 295:     def location(self) -> HexCoord:
 296:         # Planet.location is local system coords; FMS orders execute at
 297:         # the planet's global hex. Use the planet's owner's galaxy lookup
 298:         # if the planet exposes a global hex shortcut; otherwise fall
 299:         # back to ``location`` (tests that wrap a free-standing planet
 300:         # set ``location`` directly to the global hex).
 301:         gh = getattr(self._planet, "global_hex", None)
 302:         if gh is not None:
 303:             return gh  # type: ignore[no-any-return]
 304:         return self._planet.location
 305: 
 306:     @property
 307:     def owner_id(self) -> int:
 308:         return int(self._planet.owner_id) if self._planet.owner_id is not None else -1
 309: 
 310:     @property
 311:     def display_label(self) -> str:
 312:         name = getattr(self._planet, "name", None)
 313:         if name:
 314:             return f"Planet {name}"
 315:         return f"Planet {getattr(self._planet, 'id', '?')}"
 316: 
 317:     def pop_carried(
 318:         self,
 319:         vehicle_type: str,
 320:         design_id: Optional[str],
 321:         count: Optional[int],
 322:     ) -> List[Any]:
 323:         yard = self._planet.staging_yard
 324:         popped: List[Any] = []
 325:         remaining: List[Any] = []
 326:         limit = count if (count is not None and count >= 0) else None
 327:         for item in yard:
 328:             if limit is not None and len(popped) >= limit:
 329:                 remaining.append(item)
 330:                 continue
 331:             if _matches(item, vehicle_type, design_id):
 332:                 popped.append(item)
 333:             else:
 334:                 remaining.append(item)
 335:         self._planet._staging_yard = remaining
 336:         return popped
 337: 
 338:     def count_carried(
 339:         self,
 340:         vehicle_type: str,
 341:         design_id: Optional[str],
 342:     ) -> int:
 343:         return sum(
 344:             1 for item in self._planet.staging_yard
 345:             if _matches(item, vehicle_type, design_id)
 346:         )
 347: 
 348:     def append_carried(self, items: List[Any]) -> int:
 349:         """Append items, respecting ``max_staging_mass`` if set.
 350: 
 351:         Honours partial-success: stops at the first item that would
 352:         overflow capacity and returns the count actually appended.
 353:         """
 354:         appended = 0
 355:         for item in items:
 356:             ok = self._planet.add_to_staging_yard(item)
 357:             if not ok:
 358:                 break
 359:             appended += 1
 360:         return appended
 361: 
 362:     def append_recovered(self, vehicle: CarriedVehicle) -> bool:
 363:         return bool(self._planet.add_to_staging_yard(vehicle.to_dict()))
 364:

 367:     "IIssuerAdapter",
 368:     "FleetShipIssuerAdapter",
 369:     "PlanetStagingYardIssuerAdapter",
 370: ]
FILE: C:\Dev\Starship Battles\game\strategy\data\ship_consumable_manager.py
   1: """
   2: ShipConsumableManager - Resource management for ShipInstance.
   3: 
   4: Extracted from ShipInstance (PROJ-87) to centralize resource logic:
   5: - Fuel/energy/ammo tracking and consumption
   6: - Per-hex and per-turn cost calculations
   7: - Warp resource costs
   8: - Resupply operations
   9: 
  10: The manager operates on the ShipInstance's consumable_levels dict and
  11: delegates stat lookups to the ship's get_calculated_stats() method.
  12: """
  13: from typing import Dict, TYPE_CHECKING
  14: 
  15: if TYPE_CHECKING:
  16:     from game.strategy.data.ship_instance import ShipInstance
  17: 
  18: 
  19: class ShipConsumableManager:
  20:     """
  21:     Manages resource tracking and consumption for a ShipInstance.
  22: 
  23:     This class centralizes all resource-related logic that was previously
  24:     duplicated across ShipInstance methods. It operates on the ship's
  25:     consumable_levels dict and gets max values from calculated stats.
  26: 
  27:     Attributes:
  28:         _ship: Reference to the owning ShipInstance.
  29:     """
  30: 
  31:     def __init__(self, ship_instance: 'ShipInstance') -> None:
  32:         """
  33:         Initialize resource manager with a reference to the owning ship.
  34: 
  35:         Args:
  36:             ship_instance: The ShipInstance this manager operates on.
  37:         """
  38:         self._ship = ship_instance
  39: 
  40:     # --- Generic Resource Methods ---
  41: 
  42:     def get_resource_capacity(self, resource_type: str) -> float:
  43:         """
  44:         Get maximum capacity for any resource type.
  45: 
  46:         Args:
  47:             resource_type: Resource type (e.g., 'fuel', 'energy', 'ammo')
  48: 
  49:         Returns:
  50:             Maximum capacity for the resource, or 0 if not available.
  51:         """
  52:         stats = self._ship.get_calculated_stats()
  53:         resource_storage = stats.get('resource_storage', {})
  54:         return resource_storage.get(resource_type, 0)
  55: 
  56:     def get_current_resource(self, resource_type: str) -> float:
  57:         """
  58:         Get current level of any resource type.
  59: 
  60:         Args:
  61:             resource_type: Resource type (e.g., 'fuel', 'energy', 'ammo')
  62: 
  63:         Returns:
  64:             Current resource level. Returns 0.0 if not tracked (safe fallback).
  65:         """
  66:         return self._ship.consumable_levels.get(resource_type, 0.0)
  67: 
  68:     def consume_resource(self, resource_type: str, amount: float) -> bool:
  69:         """
  70:         Consume a specified amount of any resource type.
  71: 
  72:         Args:
  73:             resource_type: Resource type to consume
  74:             amount: Amount to consume (must be >= 0)
  75: 
  76:         Returns:
  77:             True if resource was available and consumed, False if insufficient
  78:             or if amount is negative.
  79:         """
  80:         # Reject negative amounts - cannot "consume" a negative amount
  81:         if amount < 0:
  82:             return False
  83: 
  84:         current = self._ship.consumable_levels.get(resource_type, 0.0)
  85: 
  86:         if current < amount:
  87:             return False
  88: 
  89:         self._ship.consumable_levels[resource_type] = current - amount
  90:         return True
  91: 
  92:     # --- Cost Calculation Methods ---
  93: 
  94:     def get_all_resource_costs_per_hex(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
  95:         """
  96:         Get all per-hex consumption costs.
  97: 
  98:         Returns:
  99:             Dict mapping resource type to cost per hex of movement.
 100:         """
 101:         stats = self._ship.get_calculated_stats()
 102:         return stats.get('resource_consumption_per_hex', {})
 103: 
 104:     def get_all_resource_costs_per_turn(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
 105:         """
 106:         Get all per-turn consumption costs.
 107: 
 108:         Returns:
 109:             Dict mapping resource type to cost per turn.
 110:         """
 111:         stats = self._ship.get_calculated_stats()
 112:         return stats.get('resource_consumption_per_turn', {})
 113: 
 114:     def get_warp_resource_costs(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
 115:         """
 116:         Get all resource costs for a warp jump.
 117: 
 118:         Returns:
 119:             Dict mapping resource type to cost per warp jump.
 120:         """
 121:         stats = self._ship.get_calculated_stats()
 122:         return stats.get('warp_resource_costs', {})
 123: 
 124:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 125:     # PROJ-436 Phase 3b: stable setter / replace / snapshot API.
 126:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 127:     # External callers (``ShipInstanceWriteService``,
 128:     # ``ShipInstanceFactory``, ``ShipInstanceBridge``,
 129:     # ``ShipInstanceSerializer``) write through these methods instead of
 130:     # poking ``ship.consumable_levels[...]`` directly. Phase 3f flips the
 131:     # durable substrate to ``Container``; routing every write through
 132:     # these methods means the cutover only has to touch the bodies here.
 133: 
 134:     def set_level(self, resource_type: str, level: float) -> None:
 135:         """Set a resource's current level (uncapped â€” caller validates).
 136: 
 137:         Phase 3b stable write API. The cap-aware path is
 138:         :meth:`resupply` (which respects ``resource_storage`` capacity);
 139:         use :meth:`set_level` when the caller has already computed the
 140:         clamped value (e.g. battle-end resource capture, deserialization,
 141:         factory initialization).
 142:         """
 143:         self._ship.consumable_levels[resource_type] = float(level)
 144: 
 145:     def replace_levels(self, levels: Dict[str, float]) -> None:
 146:         """Replace the full consumable-levels dict with ``levels``.
 147: 
 148:         Used by ``ShipInstanceBridge.update_from_ship`` after a battle to
 149:         replace levels wholesale with the post-battle snapshot from the
 150:         simulation ship. The provided dict is copied to avoid aliasing.
 151:         """
 152:         self._ship._consumable_levels = {
 153:             k: float(v) for k, v in levels.items()
 154:         }
 155: 
 156:     def get_all_levels(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
 157:         """Return a copy of the full consumable-levels dict.
 158: 
 159:         Phase 3b stable read API. The returned dict is a snapshot â€”
 160:         mutations on the caller's copy do not propagate back.
 161:         """
 162:         return dict(self._ship.consumable_levels)
 163: 
 164:     # --- Resupply Methods ---
 165: 
 166:     def resupply(self, resource_name: str, amount: float) -> float:
 167:         """
 168:         Resupply a resource.
 169: 
 170:         Args:
 171:             resource_name: Resource type to resupply
 172:             amount: Amount to add
 173: 
 174:         Returns:
 175:             The actual amount resupplied.
 176:         """
 177:         max_val = self.get_resource_capacity(resource_name)
 178:         old_val = self._ship.consumable_levels.get(resource_name, 0.0)
 179:         new_val = min(max_val, old_val + amount)
 180:         self._ship.consumable_levels[resource_name] = new_val
 181:         return new_val - old_val

  46: class ShipInstance:
  47:     """
  48:     Represents a ship in the strategy layer.
  49: 
  50:     Bridges between:
  51:     - Ship designs (templates from Ship Builder)
  52:     - Strategy fleet management
  53:     - Battle simulation
  54: 
  55:     PROJ-438 Phase 3 categorization (post-PROJ-436 Phase 9). The class
  56:     is intentionally large because of D2 default (a) â€” keep inline
  57:     ``design_data`` and the explicitly-retained shim entry points
  58:     documented in PROJ-425 Phase 5d/5e. The 910-caller entry-point sweep
  59:     is OUT of PROJ-438 scope. Ratchets live at
  60:     ``tests/unit/strategy/ship_instance/test_post_container_surface.py``.
  61: 
  62:     **Owned identity** (immutable across the ship's life):
  63: 
  64:         - ``instance_id`` (uuid), ``design_id``, ``name``, ``owner_id``,
  65:           ``serial`` (per-design-per-empire)
  66: 
  67:     **Owned durable state** (inline by design):
  68: 
  69:         - ``design_data``  â€” full serialized ship template; D2 (a) keeps
  70:           this inline instead of design-lookup-by-id
  71: 
  72:     **Owned runtime state** (private storage backing the manager APIs):
  73: 
  74:         - ``_consumable_levels`` / ``_cargo_contents``  â€” private dicts;
  75:           backward-compat ``consumable_levels`` / ``cargo_contents``
  76:           properties below are the read view; canonical writes route
  77:           through ``_resource_mgr`` / ``_cargo_mgr``
  78:         - ``bay_inventory``  â€” typed two-slot carried inventory (replaces
  79:           legacy ``carried_items`` per PROJ-431 Phase 1f; legacy
  80:           property + test shim deleted in PROJ-436 Phase 9)
  81:         - ``component_toggles``, ``activation_states``, ``components``
  82:         - ``current_hp``, ``experience``, ``kills``, ``battles_survived``
  83: 
  84:     **Status flags**:
  85: 
  86:         - ``is_alive``, ``is_derelict``, ``is_operational``,
  87:           ``design_role`` / ``role_override``
  88: 
  89:     **Cached / DI slots** (not durable state):
  90: 
  91:         - ``_cached_stats``  â€” invalidated on damage / repair / DI changes
  92:         - ``_registries``    â€” injected via ``set_registries``
  93: 
  94:     **Delegate manager slots** (PROJ-425 Phase 4 canonical names):
  95: 
  96:         - ``_resource_mgr`` (``ShipConsumableManager``)
  97:         - ``_cargo_mgr`` (``ShipCargoManager``)
  98:         - ``_display_fmt`` (``ShipDisplayFormatter``)
  99:         - ``_bridge`` (``ShipInstanceBridge``)
 100: 
 101:     **Protocol-alias properties** (``IShipInstance`` compliance, PROJ-193):
 102: 
 103:         - ``design_name``, ``hull_class``, ``ship_name``, ``serial_number``,
 104:           ``effective_role``
 105: 
 106:     **Retained-shim entry points** (explicitly kept, NOT to be removed
 107:     without the 910-caller sweep â€” PROJ-425 Phase 5d/5e):
 108: 
 109:         - **Serializer shims**: ``to_dict``, ``from_dict``, ``to_json``,
 110:           ``from_json``, ``clone``  (~18 callers)
 111:         - **Bridge shims**: ``to_ship``, ``update_from_ship``  (~10
 112:           callers)
 113:         - **Resource-manager shims**: ``consume_resource``,
 114:           ``get_resource_capacity``, ``get_current_resource``,
 115:           ``get_all_resource_costs_per_hex``,
 116:           ``get_all_resource_costs_per_turn``, ``get_warp_resource_costs``,
 117:           ``resupply``
 118:         - **Write-service shims**: ``set_component_enabled``, ``repair``
 119:         - **Backward-compat property shims**: ``consumable_levels`` /
 120:           ``cargo_contents`` (post-Phase-3f read view); ``carried_items``
 121:           removed in PROJ-436 Phase 9
 122:         - **Legacy-kwargs constructor wrapper**:
 123:           ``_ship_instance_init_with_legacy_kwargs`` at module scope â€”
 124:           retained per PROJ-443 Phase 5b after 18-file audit
 125:     """
 126: 
 127:     instance_id: str  # Unique across game
 128:     design_id: str    # Reference to ship design file/name
 129:     name: str         # Instance name (may differ from design)
 130:     owner_id: int     # Empire that owns this ship
 131: 
 132:     # Design data (full serialized ship template)
 133:     design_data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
 134: 
 135:     # Current state (may differ from design defaults)
 136:     # None values mean "use design default"
 137:     current_hp: Optional[int] = None
 138: 
 139:     # PROJ-436 Phase 3f: ``consumable_levels`` and ``cargo_contents``
 140:     # are no longer dataclass fields. They survive as backward-compatible
 141:     # write-through ``@property`` accessors over the private
 142:     # ``_consumable_levels`` / ``_cargo_contents`` dict fields below
 143:     # â€” preserving test infrastructure that still pokes
 144:     # ``ship.cargo_contents[k] = v`` / ``ship.consumable_levels[k] = v``
 145:     # directly. Production callers route through ``ship._resource_mgr``
 146:     # and ``ship._cargo_mgr`` (the stable manager API landed in
 147:     # sub-phase 3b). The AST guard at
 148:     # ``tests/static_guards/test_no_legacy_storage_fields.py`` pins
 149:     # the absence of the dataclass field names.
 150:     _consumable_levels: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
 151:     component_toggles: Dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)  # component_id -> enabled
 152:     activation_states: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)  # component_key -> activation state
 153: 
 154:     # Per-component-instance persistent state. Key format:
 155:     # `component_state_key(component_id, instance_index)` =
 156:     # `"{component_id}#{instance_index}"`. Authoritative source for
 157:     # battle round-trip (BattleSpec.components / BattleOutcome.components)
 158:     # and for per-instance HP in stat calculation. PROJ-269 Phase 2 +
 159:     # PROJ-276 (closed the transition; removed the legacy `component_damage`
 160:     # dict).
 161:     components: Dict[str, ComponentState] = field(default_factory=dict)
 162: 
 163:     # PROJ-436 Phase 3f: see comment on ``_consumable_levels`` above.
 164:     _cargo_contents: Dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
 165: 
 166:     # PROJ-431 Phase 1f: typed two-slot carried inventory replaces the
 167:     # legacy ``carried_items: List[Dict[str, Any]]`` mixed-shape list.
 168:     #
 169:     # ``bay_inventory.bay`` holds homogeneous ``CarriedVehicle`` entries
 170:     # (mines / fighters / satellites). ``bay_inventory.pods`` holds
 171:     # ``DropPod`` entries. The previous runtime
 172:     # ``CarriedVehicle.from_any(...)`` discriminator was needed only to
 173:     # walk the mixed-shape list and is now removed.
 174:     #
 175:     # A backward-compatible ``carried_items`` *property* (NOT a dataclass
 176:     # field) is kept below for test infrastructure that still pokes the
 177:     # legacy dict-list shape â€” see ``carried_items`` property.
 178:     bay_inventory: BayInventory = field(default_factory=BayInventory)
 179: 
 180:     # Status
 181:     is_alive: bool = True
 182:     is_derelict: bool = False
 183:     is_operational: bool = True
 184: 
 185:     # Design role classification
 186:     design_role: Optional[str] = None    # Auto-classified role (DesignRole value)
 187:     role_override: Optional[str] = None  # Player override (DesignRole value)
 188: 
 189:     # Strategy tracking
 190:     experience: int = 0           # For future crew/veteran system
 191:     kills: int = 0
 192:     battles_survived: int = 0
 193: 
 194:     # Serial number - unique per design within an empire
 195:     serial: Optional[int] = None
 196: 
 197:     # Cached calculated stats (invalidated on damage change)
 198:     _cached_stats: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default=None, repr=False)
 199: 
 200:     # PROJ-211: Injected registries for stats calculation (no global fallback)
 201:     _registries: Optional['GameRegistries'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
 202: 
 203:     # Delegate managers (initialized in __post_init__).
 204:     # PROJ-425 Phase 4: canonical names on the entity are `_resource_mgr`
 205:     # / `_cargo_mgr` / `_display_fmt` / `_bridge`. The write service was
 206:     # querying `_cargo_manager` / `_consumable_manager` (dead code, since
 207:     # those attributes never existed); fixed in this phase to match the
 208:     # entity instead of renaming ~50 callers across production + tests.
 209:     _resource_mgr: Optional['ShipConsumableManager'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
 210:     _cargo_mgr: Optional['ShipCargoManager'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
 211:     _display_fmt: Optional['ShipDisplayFormatter'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
 212:     _bridge: Optional['ShipInstanceBridge'] = field(default=None, repr=False, init=False)
 213: 
 214:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
 215:         """Initialize delegate managers after dataclass init."""
 216:         self._resource_mgr = ShipConsumableManager(self)
 217:         self._cargo_mgr = ShipCargoManager(self)
 218:         self._display_fmt = ShipDisplayFormatter(self)
 219:         self._bridge = ShipInstanceBridge(self)
 220: 
 221:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 222:     # PROJ-449 Phase 4: read-only views over private consumable / cargo
 223:     # storage. The matching @setter shims were retired together with
 224:     # the legacy-kwarg constructor wrapper. See PROJ-449 decisions.md
 225:     # row 2026-05-18 "Phase 3+4 scope adjustment".
 226:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 227: 
 228:     @property
 229:     def consumable_levels(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
 230:         """Read-only view over private consumable storage.
 231: 
 232:         Writes must route through ``_resource_mgr.set_level`` /
 233:         ``replace_levels`` / ``deplete``.
 234:         """
 235:         return self._consumable_levels
 236: 
 237:     @property
 238:     def cargo_contents(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
 239:         """Read-only view over private cargo storage.
 240: 
 241:         PROJ-446 Phase 2 narrowed the protocol annotation to
 242:         ``Mapping[str, int]``. Phase 5 of PROJ-449 will drop the
 243:         "not read-only in absolute terms" caveat from the protocol
 244:         docstring now that the concrete-class @setter is gone.
 245:         Writes must route through ``_cargo_mgr.set_cargo`` /
 246:         ``add_to_cargo`` / ``remove_from_cargo``.
 247:         """
 248:         return self._cargo_contents
 249: 
 250:     def set_registries(self, registries: 'GameRegistries') -> None:
 251:         """
 252:         Set the registries for stats calculation.
 253: 
 254:         PROJ-211: Allows setting registries after construction for objects
 255:         created without registries (e.g., deserialization).
 256: 
 257:         Args:
 258:             registries: GameRegistries instance for stats calculation.
 259:         """
 260:         self._registries = registries
 261:         self.invalidate_stats_cache()
 262: 
 263:     # PROJ-193: Property aliases for IShipInstance Protocol compliance
 264:     @property
 265:     def design_name(self) -> str:
 266:         """Design name from design_data (IShipInstance Protocol)."""
 267:         return self.design_data.get('name', self.design_id)
 268: 
 269:     @property
 270:     def hull_class(self) -> str:
 271:         """Ship's hull class from design_data (IShipInstance Protocol)."""
 272:         return self.design_data.get('ship_class', 'Unknown')
 273: 
 274:     @property
 275:     def ship_name(self) -> str:
 276:         """Instance name alias (IShipInstance Protocol)."""
 277:         return self.name
 278: 
 279:     @property
 280:     def serial_number(self) -> Optional[int]:
 281:         """Serial number alias (IShipInstance Protocol)."""
 282:         return self.serial
 283: 
 284:     @property
 285:     def effective_role(self) -> Optional[str]:
 286:         """The active role: role_override if set, else design_role."""
 287:         if self.role_override is not None:
 288:             return self.role_override
 289:         return self.design_role
 290: 
 291:     def __hash__(self) -> int:
 292:         return hash(self.instance_id)
 293: 
 294:     def __eq__(self, other: object) -> bool:
 295:         if not isinstance(other, ShipInstance):
 296:             return NotImplemented
 297:         return self.instance_id == other.instance_id
 298: 
 299:     @classmethod
 300:     def create(
 301:         cls,
 302:         design_data: Dict[str, Any],
 303:         owner_id: int,
 304:         name: Optional[str] = None,
 305:         design_id: Optional[str] = None,
 306:         empire: Optional['Empire'] = None,
 307:         registries: Optional['GameRegistries'] = None,
 308:     ) -> 'ShipInstance':
 309:         """Create a new ship instance from a design.
 310: 
 311:         Thin shim around `ShipInstanceFactory.create` (PROJ-425 Phase 3).
 312:         The shim remains until grep shows no callers still using
 313:         `ShipInstance.create(...)` directly.
 314: 
 315:         Args:
 316:             design_data: Full ship design dictionary
 317:                 (from `ShipSerializer.to_dict()`).
 318:             owner_id: Empire that owns this ship.
 319:             name: Instance name (defaults to design name).
 320:             design_id: Design identifier (defaults to design name).
 321:             empire: Empire to draw the serial number from. If None, no
 322:                 serial is assigned and a warning is logged.
 323:             registries: `GameRegistries` for stats calculation. Required
 324:                 for proper DI. Without it, `get_calculated_stats()` raises.
 325: 
 326:         Returns:
 327:             New `ShipInstance` with unique `instance_id`.
 328:         """
 329:         from game.strategy.services.ship_instance_factory import ShipInstanceFactory
 330:         return ShipInstanceFactory.create(
 331:             design_data=design_data,
 332:             owner_id=owner_id,
 333:             name=name,
 334:             design_id=design_id,
 335:             empire=empire,
 336:             registries=registries,
 337:         )
 338: 
 339:     def is_damaged(self) -> bool:
 340:         """Check if ship has any damage â€” hull, per-component, or derelict."""
 341:         return (
 342:             self.current_hp is not None or
 343:             any(cs.is_damaged for cs in self.components.values()) or
 344:             self.is_derelict
 345:         )
 346: 
 347:     def is_combat_capable(self) -> bool:
 348:         """Check if ship can participate in combat."""
 349:         return self.is_alive and not self.is_derelict
 350: 
 351:     def get_activation_state(self, component_key: str) -> 'ComponentActivationState':
 352:         """Get the activation state for a component."""
 353:         from game.strategy.data.component_activation_state import ComponentActivationState
 354:         data = self.activation_states.get(component_key)
 355:         if data is None:
 356:             return ComponentActivationState()
 357:         if isinstance(data, dict):
 358:             return ComponentActivationState.from_dict(data)
 359:         return ComponentActivationState()
 360: 
 361:     def set_activation_state(self, component_key: str, state: 'ComponentActivationState') -> None:
 362:         """Store the activation state for a component."""
 363:         self.activation_states[component_key] = state.to_dict()
 364: 
 365:     def get_calculated_stats(self, force_refresh: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
 366:         """
 367:         Get calculated stats from components, respecting damage state.
 368: 
 369:         Delegates to `ShipStatsCache` (PROJ-425 Phase 1): the helper owns
 370:         the registry-DI calculation path and the cache rule. Storage of
 371:         `_cached_stats` remains on this entity per TD-06 Guardrail #2.
 372: 
 373:         Args:
 374:             force_refresh: If True, recalculate even if cached
 375: 
 376:         Returns:
 377:             Dict with calculated stats (max_hp, mass, resource_storage, etc.)
 378:         """
 379:         from game.strategy.data.ship_stats_cache import ShipStatsCache
 380:         return ShipStatsCache.get_or_compute(self, force_refresh=force_refresh)
 381: 
 382:     def invalidate_stats_cache(self) -> None:
 383:         """
 384:         Invalidate cached stats.
 385: 
 386:         Call this when component damage changes, after battle,
 387:         or after repair operations.
 388:         """
 389:         from game.strategy.data.ship_stats_cache import ShipStatsCache
 390:         ShipStatsCache.invalidate(self)
 391: 
 392:     def get_hp_percentage(self) -> float:
 393:         """Get current HP as percentage of max."""
 394:         if self.current_hp is None:
 395:             return 1.0
 396:         max_hp = self.get_calculated_stats().get('max_hp', _DEFAULT_MAX_HP)
 397:         if max_hp <= 0:
 398:             return 0.0
 399:         return self.current_hp / max_hp
 400: 
 401:     def get_resource_percentage(self, resource_name: str) -> float:
 402:         """Get current resource level as percentage of max."""
 403:         return self._display_fmt.get_resource_percentage(resource_name)
 404: 
 405:     # --- Generic Resource Methods (PROJ-425 Phase 5b: thin shims) ---
 406:     #
 407:     # These remain as forwarders because too many tests mock them as
 408:     # entity-level methods (e.g. `ship.consume_resource = Mock(...)`).
 409:     # Migrating those mocks is a bigger change than the demolition saves.
 410:     # The canonical implementations live on `ShipConsumableManager`.
 411: 
 412:     def get_resource_capacity(self, resource_type: str) -> float:
 413:         """Get maximum capacity for a resource type (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
 414:         return self._resource_mgr.get_resource_capacity(resource_type)
 415: 
 416:     def get_current_resource(self, resource_type: str) -> float:
 417:         """Get current level of a resource type (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
 418:         return self._resource_mgr.get_current_resource(resource_type)
 419: 
 420:     def consume_resource(self, resource_type: str, amount: float) -> bool:
 421:         """Consume a resource amount (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
 422:         return self._resource_mgr.consume_resource(resource_type, amount)
 423: 
 424:     def get_all_resource_costs_per_hex(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
 425:         """Per-hex consumption costs (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
 426:         return self._resource_mgr.get_all_resource_costs_per_hex()
 427: 
 428:     def get_all_resource_costs_per_turn(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
 429:         """Per-turn consumption costs (shim to `_resource_mgr`)."""
 430:         return self._resource_mgr.get_all_resource_costs_per_turn()
 431: 
 432:     # --- Cargo / carried-vehicle helpers ---
 433:     #
 434:     # PROJ-425 Phase 6 (TD-06 batch 5c): the method forwarders for
 435:     # cargo queries / mutators, carried-vehicle queries, and pod-storage
 436:     # helpers were demolished. Callers go through ``ship._cargo_mgr``
 437:     # (see :class:`ShipCargoManager`). The ``bay_current_mass`` and
 438:     # ``bay_capacity_mass`` *properties* below are kept as small
 439:     # read-only entity attributes â€” they read naturally as
 440:     # ship-instance state (parallel to ``design_name`` /
 441:     # ``hull_class``) and are referenced by tests as such.
 442: 
 443:     @property
 444:     def bay_current_mass(self) -> float:
 445:         """PROJ-FMS-A: runtime vehicle-bay usage on this ship.
 446: 
 447:         Mirrors the ``bay_capacity_mass`` design stat. Computed at the
 448:         strategy layer because it depends on the actual contents of
 449:         ``bay_inventory.bay`` (simulation ``Ship`` cannot see those).
 450:         """
 451:         return self._cargo_mgr.get_carried_vehicle_mass()
 452: 
 453:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 454:     # PROJ-436 Phase 3a â€” unified Container projections.
 455:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 456:     #
 457:     # These are read-only snapshot views over the legacy
 458:     # `consumable_levels` and `cargo_contents` dataclass fields. Later
 459:     # sub-phases of Phase 3 migrate callers off the legacy fields; the
 460:     # final cutover deletes both fields and these views become the
 461:     # canonical read API. Until then, the views serve callers that
 462:     # want to work in the unified Container abstraction without
 463:     # forcing a wholesale migration.
 464:     #
 465:     # Snapshot semantics: mutations on the returned Container are NOT
 466:     # propagated back to the ship. Treat as a read view.
 467: 
 468:     def consumable_container(self) -> 'Container':
 469:         """Container snapshot of this ship's `consumable_levels`.
 470: 
 471:         Every entry in `consumable_levels` (resource_name -> amount)
 472:         appears in the resource slice. Unknown resource ids (not in
 473:         `ResourceCatalog`) raise via `Container.add_resource` â€”
 474:         consumable_levels keys are assumed to be canonical resource ids
 475:         per the existing component-driven loading code.
 476:         """
 477:         from game.strategy.data.container import (
 478:             Container,
 479:             ContainerPolicy,
 480:         )
 481:         from game.strategy.data.containable import (
 482:             ContainableKind,
 483:             ResourceContainable,
 484:         )
 485: 
 486:         policy = ContainerPolicy(
 487:             allowed_kinds=frozenset({ContainableKind.RESOURCE}),
 488:             allowed_type_ids=None,
 489:         )
 490:         c = Container(capacity_mass=float("inf"), policy=policy)
 491:         for resource_id, amount in self.consumable_levels.items():
 492:             c.add(ResourceContainable(resource_id), amount)
 493:         return c
 494: 
 495:     def cargo_container(self) -> 'Container':
 496:         """Container snapshot of this ship's `cargo_contents`.
 497: 
 498:         Translation rules for cargo_contents keys:
 499:         - "passengers" -> population slice (default species id).
 500:         - Known resource ids (in `ResourceCatalog`) -> resource slice.
 501:         - Other keys -> logged and skipped (no representation in the
 502:           snapshot). The most common skipped keys are "drop_pod" and
 503:           "vehicle" which live in `bay_inventory` instead.
 504:         """
 505:         from game.core.resources import ResourceCatalog
 506:         from game.strategy.data.container import (
 507:             Container,
 508:             ContainerPolicy,
 509:             _get_resource_catalog,
 510:         )
 511:         from game.strategy.data.containable import (
 512:             ContainableKind,
 513:             PopulationContainable,
 514:             ResourceContainable,
 515:         )
 516: 
 517:         catalog: ResourceCatalog = _get_resource_catalog()
 518:         policy = ContainerPolicy(
 519:             allowed_kinds=frozenset({
 520:                 ContainableKind.RESOURCE,
 521:                 ContainableKind.POPULATION,
 522:             }),
 523:             allowed_type_ids=None,
 524:         )
 525:         c = Container(capacity_mass=float("inf"), policy=policy)
 526:         for key, amount in self.cargo_contents.items():
 527:             if key == "passengers":
 528:                 c.add(PopulationContainable("default"), int(amount))
 529:             elif catalog.has(key):
 530:                 c.add(ResourceContainable(key), float(amount))
 531:             else:
 532:                 logger.debug(
 533:                     "cargo_container: skipping cargo_contents key %r "
 534:                     "(not a known resource id or 'passengers')",
 535:                     key,
 536:                 )
 537:         return c
 538: 
 539:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 540:     # PROJ-431 Phase 1f: bay_inventory IS the canonical storage.
 541:     # ------------------------------------------------------------------
 542:     #
 543:     # The typed :class:`BayInventory` (``bay: list[CarriedVehicle]`` +
 544:     # ``pods: list[DropPod]``) is now a real dataclass field on this
 545:     # entity, not a projection over the legacy mixed-shape list. The
 546:     # earlier ``carried_items`` field was removed; what remains here is
 547:     # a backward-compatible property/setter exposing the legacy
 548:     # dict-list shape exclusively for **test infrastructure** that still
 549:     # pokes ``ship.carried_items.append({...})`` directly. Production
 550:     # code uses :attr:`bay_inventory` and :meth:`set_bay_inventory`.
 551: 
 552:     def set_bay_inventory(self, bay_inventory: 'BayInventory') -> None:
 553:         """Replace this ship's typed bay inventory wholesale.
 554: 
 555:         Phase 1f: this is a thin attribute setter retained as a stable
 556:         write surface for callers that produced a new
 557:         :class:`BayInventory` value (e.g. ``ShipCargoManager`` loaders /
 558:         unloaders, FMS handlers). Callers that just mutate the lists in
 559:         place do not need to call this â€” the underlying field is now
 560:         owned by this entity directly.
 561:         """
 562:         if not isinstance(bay_inventory, BayInventory):
 563:             raise TypeError(
 564:                 f"set_bay_inventory expects BayInventory, got "
 565:                 f"{type(bay_inventory).__name__}"
 566:             )
 567:         self.bay_inventory = bay_inventory
 568: 
 569:     # PROJ-436 Phase 9: ``carried_items`` property + ``_CarriedItemsProxy``
 570:     # test shim deleted. Callers route through ``bay_inventory.bay`` /
 571:     # ``bay_inventory.pods`` / ``set_bay_inventory(...)`` directly.
 572: 
 573:     @property
 574:     def bay_capacity_mass(self) -> float:
 575:         """PROJ-FMS-A: maximum vehicle-bay capacity from design stats."""
 576:         _current, max_mass = self._cargo_mgr.get_vehicle_bay_capacity()
 577:         return max_mass
 578: 
 579:     # PROJ-425 Phase 6 (TD-06 batch 5c): pod-storage helpers

  20: class Empire:
  21:     """
  22:     Represents a player or AI faction.
  23:     """
  24:     def __init__(self, empire_id, name, color, theme_path=None, empire_theme_id="Federation",
  25:                  flag_id: str = "", portrait_id: str = "", race_config=None):
  26:         self.id = empire_id
  27:         self.name = name
  28:         self.color = color
  29:         self.theme_path = theme_path
  30:         self.empire_theme_id = empire_theme_id  # Ship theme for this empire's designs
  31:         # Race visual identity (from RaceConfig selection during game setup)
  32:         self.flag_id = flag_id  # Custom flag directory (e.g., "flag_2fl0bh2fl0bh2fl0")
  33:         self.portrait_id = portrait_id  # Race portrait filename
  34:         # Full race configuration for habitability/growth calculations
  35:         self.race_config = race_config  # RaceConfig or None
  36: 
  37:         self.colonies = [] # List[Planet]
  38:         self.fleets = [] # List[Fleet]
  39:         # PROJ-431 Phase 2: deployed groups (typed sibling-of-Fleet) live in
  40:         # their own collection. ``MineGroup`` is the first concrete subclass;
  41:         # ``FighterWing`` / ``SatelliteConstellation`` arrive in Phase 3.
  42:         self.deployed_groups: List[DeployedGroup] = []
  43: 
  44:         # Design library tracking (Phase 3)
  45:         self.designed_ships = []  # List[DesignMetadata] - cached design list
  46:         self.built_ship_designs = set()  # Set of design_ids that were ever built
  47: 
  48:         # Fleet display number generator (per-empire sequential, cosmetic only)
  49:         self._next_fleet_display_number = 1
  50: 
  51:         # Ship serial number counters - per design_id
  52:         self._design_serial_counters = {}  # Dict[str, int]
  53: 
  54:         # Galaxy back-reference for auto fleet registration (PROJ-219)
  55:         self._galaxy: Optional['Galaxy'] = None
  56: 
  57:         # Empire-wide resource economy.
  58:         # ``resource_pool`` is a pure aggregation query over
  59:         # ``self.colonies[*].stockpile`` (see property below). There is
  60:         # no durable empire-level resource store; fleet construction
  61:         # draws from the build-location's container (planet stockpile
  62:         # or fleet cargo).
  63:         self.max_storage = {}     # Dict[str, float] - aggregate storage capacity (set by HarvestingEngine)
  64: 
  65:         # PROJ-412 Phase 5: transient dirty flags consumed by HarvestingEngine's
  66:         # per-turn caches. NOT serialized â€” flipped to True at construction and
  67:         # on every mid-turn mutation that could change storage or booster
  68:         # contributions; cleared by the engine after it rebuilds its cache.
  69:         # Start True so the first turn always rebuilds.
  70:         self._storage_dirty: bool = True
  71:         self._booster_dirty: bool = True
  72: 
  73:     def is_eliminated(self) -> bool:
  74:         """Return True when this empire owns no fleets, no colonies, and
  75:         no deployed groups.
  76: 
  77:         Pure read; no side effects. Used by the hot-seat turn-start hook
  78:         (issue #25) to detect defeat, remove the empire from the rotation,
  79:         and fire a one-shot defeat modal. AI/non-human empires use the
  80:         same predicate.
  81: 
  82:         PROJ-431 Phase 2: deployed groups (e.g. orphan ``MineGroup``s)
  83:         keep an empire alive â€” a minefield on the map is still an asset
  84:         on the strategic surface, even after every fleet/colony is lost.
  85:         """
  86:         return (
  87:             len(self.fleets) == 0
  88:             and len(self.colonies) == 0
  89:             and len(self.deployed_groups) == 0
  90:         )
  91: 
  92:     # --- Deployed groups (PROJ-431 Phase 2) ---
  93: 
  94:     def add_deployed_group(self, group: DeployedGroup) -> None:
  95:         """Attach a deployed group to this empire.
  96: 
  97:         Takes ownership: ``group.owner_id`` is rewritten to this empire's
  98:         id, matching ``add_fleet``'s contract. Dedupes â€” adding the same
  99:         group twice is a no-op.
 100:         """
 101:         if group in self.deployed_groups:
 102:             return
 103:         self.deployed_groups.append(group)
 104:         group.owner_id = self.id
 105: 
 106:     def remove_deployed_group(self, group: DeployedGroup) -> bool:
 107:         """Detach a deployed group. Returns True if it was present."""
 108:         if group in self.deployed_groups:
 109:             self.deployed_groups.remove(group)
 110:             return True
 111:         return False
 112: 
 113:     def deployed_groups_of(self, cls: Type[_DG]) -> List[_DG]:
 114:         """Filter ``deployed_groups`` to a single concrete type.
 115: 
 116:         The PROJ-431 design.md "IDeployedGroupSource accessor" â€” AI

 142:     def add_fleet(self, fleet) -> None:

 177:                             EventType.FLEET_JOIN_CANCELLED,
 178:                             category=EventCategory.FLEET_OPERATIONS,
 179:                             empire_id=pursuer.owner_id,
 180:                             message=f"Fleet {pursuer.id} join order cancelled: target Fleet {fleet.id} destroyed",

 189:     def get_next_fleet_display_number(self) -> int:

 201:     def get_next_serial(self, design_id: str) -> int:

 210:         Returns:
 211:             Next serial number for this design (1, 2, 3, ...)
 212:         """

 214:         next_serial = current + 1

 216:         return next_serial
